I don’t think I’ve been feeling ill in a way that would explain it, but I’m not really sure what else to look for.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling down, hopeless, or losing pleasure in things you used to enjoy lately?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I don’t think I’ve been hopeless, exactly. I still do my usual things, but I haven’t paid much attention to whether I’m enjoying them less.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that activities you used to enjoy don’t give you as much pleasure as they used to?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I still do my usual things, like getting outside, but I haven’t thought much about whether they feel less enjoyable than before.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that the things you used to enjoy, like gardening or walking, don’t bring you the same sense of pleasure as they used to?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure, doctor. I still do those things sometimes, like walking, but I haven’t really noticed whether they feel different or less enjoyable.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you don’t get as much pleasure or happiness from things you used to enjoy, like gardening or walking?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know. I still go for walks and do some of the usual things, but I can’t say I’ve noticed a big change in how much pleasure I get from them.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
